     1	//! Carry authored guards; never invent a value for a newly optional field.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — compilation gates automatic registration.
     3	use crate::{ast::Stmt, parser, print};
     4	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     5	
     6	// Guard meaning depends on statement order and named definitions, not merely
     7	// the last textual binding. Compare declared row identities, then select
     8	// current execution when that guarded context cannot be transported intact.
     9	// Field/table authority is validated separately before choosing a strategy.
    10	pub(super) fn current_context_needed(
    11	    sources:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)],
    12	    view_schema:&snapback4_core::Schema,current_schema:&snapback4_core::Schema,
    13	)->bool {
    14	    let view_docs=sources.iter().map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    15	    let mut next_docs=current.iter().map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    16	    let mut names=current_schema.table_renames.iter().filter(|(_,old)|view_schema.tables.contains_key(*old)).map(|(new,old)|(new.clone(),old.clone())).collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    17	    for(name,prior)in &view_schema.table_renames {
    18	        if current_schema.tables.contains_key(prior)&&!current_schema.tables.contains_key(name){names.insert(prior.clone(),name.clone());}
    19	    }
    20	    for doc in &mut next_docs{normalize_identities(doc,&names);}
    21	    if super::release_bridge_indexes::current_context_needed(&view_docs,&next_docs,view_schema,current_schema,&names){return true;}
    22	    let exports=|docs:&[crate::ast::Document]| {
    23	        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    24	        for doc in docs {
    25	            for p in &doc.programs{out.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,&p.name),p.clone());}
    26	            for(name,target,_)in &doc.aliases{if let Some(p)=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target){out.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,name),p.clone());}}
    27	        }out
    28	    };
    29	    let shapes=|docs:&[crate::ast::Document]| {
    30	        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    31	        for doc in docs {
    32	            for shape in &doc.shapes {
    33	                let fields=shape.fields.iter().map(|field|match field {
    34	                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Spread{..}=>"...".into(),
    35	                    crate::ast::ShapeField::NamedSpread{name,..}=>format!("...{name}"),
    36	                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Bare{name,..}=>name.clone(),
    37	                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Field{name,value,..}=>format!("{name}:{}",expression_signature(value)),
    38	                }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    39	                out.insert(shape.name.clone(),fields);
    40	            }
    41	        }out
    42	    };
    43	    let shapes_changed=shapes(&view_docs)!=shapes(&next_docs);
    44	    let view=exports(&view_docs);let next=exports(&next_docs);
    45	    for(name,p)in next {
    46	        let Some(old)=view.get(&name)else{continue};
    47	        let mut sensitive=p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body).any(|s|matches!(s,Stmt::Require{..}|Stmt::If{..}|Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}));
    48	        let mut doc=crate::ast::Document::default();doc.programs.push(p.clone());
    49	        crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut doc,&mut |expr|{
    50	            sensitive|=matches!(expr,crate::ast::Expr::Read(_)|crate::ast::Expr::Exists{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Created{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Walk{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Merge(_)|crate::ast::Expr::If{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Assert{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Call{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Write{condition:Some(_),..});
    51	        });
    52	        let body=|p:&crate::ast::ProgramDecl|p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body).map(normalized_statement).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    53	        if sensitive&&(body(old)!=body(&p)||shapes_changed){return true;}
    54	    }
    55	    false
    56	}

    58	// Only typed row-reference slots change spelling. Literal values, member names,
    59	// helper identities and business strings retain their authored meaning.
    60	fn normalize_identities(doc:&mut crate::ast::Document,names:&std::collections::BTreeMap<String,String>) {
    61	    fn kind(ty:&mut crate::ast::ScalarType,names:&std::collections::BTreeMap<String,String>){
    62	        match &mut ty.kind {
    63	            crate::ast::TypeKind::Ref{table}=>if let Some(old)=names.get(table){*table=old.clone();},
    64	            crate::ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>kind(item,names),
    65	            _=>(),
    66	        }
    67	    }
    68	    for p in &mut doc.programs{for a in &mut p.args{kind(&mut a.type_,names);}}
    69	    for f in &mut doc.functions{for a in &mut f.args{kind(&mut a.type_,names);}}
    70	    let table=|name:&mut String|{if let Some(old)=names.get(name){*name=old.clone();}};
    71	    crate::bind::intent_walk::document(doc,&mut |expr|match expr {
    72	        crate::ast::Expr::Read(r)|crate::ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}|crate::ast::Expr::Created{read:r,..}=>table(&mut r.table),
    73	        crate::ast::Expr::Write{table:name,..}|crate::ast::Expr::Walk{table:name,..}=>table(name),
    74	        crate::ast::Expr::Merge(m)=>{table(&mut m.table);for lane in &mut m.lanes{if let Some(name)=&mut lane.table{table(name);}if let Some(r)=&mut lane.planned{table(&mut r.table);}if let Some((_,r))=&mut lane.nulls{table(&mut r.table);}}},
    75	        _=>(),
    76	    });
    77	}
    78	
    79	pub(super) fn carry(
    80	    sources: &mut [(String, String)],
    81	    current: &[(String, String)],
    82	    _affected: &BTreeSet<String>,
    83	    changes: &mut Vec<String>,
    84	) -> (Vec<crate::Diagnostic>,BTreeSet<String>) {
    85	    let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
    86	    let mut fallback=BTreeSet::new();
    87	    let mut current_documents = current
    88	        .iter()
    89	        .map(|(file, text)| parser::parse_document(file, text).0)
    90	        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    91	    let target_documents = sources
    92	        .iter()
    93	        .map(|(file, text)| parser::parse_document(file, text).0)
    94	        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    95	    let target_tables=target_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).map(|t|t.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    96	    let current_tables=current_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).map(|t|t.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    97	    let mut identities=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    98	    for table in current_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
    99	        if let Some(prior)=table.renamed_from.as_ref().filter(|prior|target_tables.contains(*prior)){identities.insert(table.name.clone(),prior.clone());}
   100	    }
   101	    for table in target_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
   102	        if let Some(prior)=table.renamed_from.as_ref().filter(|prior|current_tables.contains(*prior)&&!current_tables.contains(&table.name)){identities.insert(prior.clone(),table.name.clone());}
   103	    }
   104	    for doc in &mut current_documents{normalize_identities(doc,&identities);}
   105	    let helper_signatures = |docs: &[crate::ast::Document]| {
   106	        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   107	        for doc in docs {
   108	            for f in &doc.functions {
   109	                let args=f.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),a.type_.clone(),a.alias.clone(),a.default.as_ref().map(expression_signature),a.deprecated)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   110	                let body=match &f.body {
   111	                    crate::ast::FunctionBody::Expression(e)=>vec![format!("expression {}",expression_signature(e))],
   112	                    crate::ast::FunctionBody::Block(body)=>body.iter().map(normalized_statement).collect(),
   113	                };
   114	                let mut function=crate::ast::Document::default();function.functions.push(f.clone());
   115	                let mut calls=Vec::new();crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut function,&mut |e|if let crate::ast::Expr::Call{name,..}=e{calls.push(name.clone());});
   116	                out.insert(f.name.clone(),((args,body),calls));
   117	            }
   118	        }out
   119	    };
   120	    let current_helpers = helper_signatures(&current_documents);
   121	    let target_helpers = helper_signatures(&target_documents);
   122	    let mut next=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   123	    for doc in &current_documents {
   124	        for p in &doc.programs {next.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,&p.name),p.clone());}
   125	        for(name,target,_)in &doc.aliases {if let Some(p)=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target){let mut exported=p.clone();exported.name=name.clone();next.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,name),exported);}}
   126	    }
   127	    for (file, text) in sources {
   128	        let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
   129	        let mut edits = Vec::new();
   130	        for old in doc.programs {
   131	            let Some(new) = next.get(&super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name)) else {
   132	                continue;
   133	            };
   134	            // The whole selected source must retain current matching guards,
   135	            // including changes unrelated to the column that triggered a view.
   136	            let guards = old
   137	                .prelude
   138	                .iter()
   139	                .chain(&old.body)
   140	                .filter_map(guard)
   141	                .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   142	            let added = new
   143	                .prelude
   144	                .iter()
   145	                .chain(&new.body)
   146	                .filter_map(guard)
   147	                .filter(|g| !guards.contains(g))
   148	                .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   149	            let bindings = |p: &crate::ast::ProgramDecl| {
   150	                p.prelude
   151	                    .iter()
   152	                    .chain(&p.body)
   153	                    .filter_map(|s| {
   154	                        if let Stmt::Bind { names, value, .. } = s {
   155	                            Some((names, value))
   156	                        } else {
   157	                            None
   158	                        }
   159	                    })
   160	                    .flat_map(|(names, value)| {
   161	                        names.iter().enumerate().map(move |(at, name)| {
   162	                            (
   163	                                name.clone(),
   164	                                (format!("{at}:{}", expression_signature(value)), value.clone()),
   165	                            )
   166	                        })
   167	                    })
   168	                    .collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_, _>>()
   169	            };
   170	            let old_bindings = bindings(&old);
   171	            let new_bindings = bindings(new);
   172	            let mut dependent = BTreeSet::new();
   173	            let mut helpers = BTreeSet::new();
   174	            let collect = |expr: &crate::ast::Expr,
   175	                           names: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   176	                           helpers: &mut BTreeSet<String>| {
   177	                let mut expr = expr.clone();
   178	                crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr, &mut |e| match e {
   179	                    crate::ast::Expr::Name { name, .. } => {
   180	                        names.insert(name.clone());
   181	                    }
   182	                    crate::ast::Expr::Call { name, .. } => {
   183	                        helpers.insert(name.clone());
   184	                    }
   185	                    _ => (),
   186	                });
   187	            };
   188	            for s in new.prelude.iter().chain(&new.body) {
   189	                if let Stmt::Require { condition, .. } = s {
   190	                    collect(condition, &mut dependent, &mut helpers);
   191	                }
   192	            }
   193	            let mut visited = BTreeSet::new();
   194	            let mut unsafe_binding = false;
   195	            while let Some(name) = dependent.pop_first() {
   196	                if !visited.insert(name.clone()) {
   197	                    continue;
   198	                }
   199	                if let Some((signature, value)) = new_bindings.get(&name) {
   200	                    if old_bindings.get(&name).map(|(s, _)| s) != Some(signature)
   201	                        || has_write(value)
   202	                    {
   203	                        unsafe_binding = true;
   204	                    }
   205	                    collect(value, &mut dependent, &mut helpers);
   206	                } else if old_bindings.contains_key(&name) {
   207	                    unsafe_binding = true;
   208	                }
   209	            }
   210	            // Reused authored workflows retain their complete helper meaning,
   211	            // including helpers feeding writes, returns and system scopes.
   212	            let mut workflow = crate::ast::Document::default();
   213	            workflow.programs.push(new.clone());
   214	            crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut workflow, &mut |e| {
   215	                if let crate::ast::Expr::Call { name, .. } = e {
   216	                    helpers.insert(name.clone());
   217	                }
   218	            });
   219	            let mut visited = BTreeSet::new();
   220	            let mut unsafe_helper = false;
   221	            while let Some(name) = helpers.pop_first() {
   222	                // Helper declarations are global after the compiler validates
   223	                // optional file qualification. Compare the resolved identity.
   224	                let name=name.rsplit('.').next().unwrap_or(&name).to_owned();
   225	                if !visited.insert(name.clone()) {
   226	                    continue;
   227	                }
   228	                if current_helpers.get(&name).map(|s| &s.0)
   229	                    != target_helpers.get(&name).map(|s| &s.0)
   230	                {
   231	                    unsafe_helper = true;
   232	                }
   233	                if let Some((_, calls)) = current_helpers.get(&name) {
   234	                    helpers.extend(calls.iter().cloned());
   235	                }
   236	            }
   237	            let nested = |p: &crate::ast::ProgramDecl| {
   238	                p.body
   239	                    .iter()
   240	                    .filter(|s| matches!(s, Stmt::If { .. } | Stmt::For { .. } | Stmt::As { .. }))

exec

     1	//! Independent policy follows the complete authored current execution closure.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — no restored workflow may bypass current independent intent.
     3	use crate::{

    14	pub(super) fn prepare(target: &[Source], current: &[Source], guarded_fallback: &mut bool) -> Bridge {
    15	    *guarded_fallback=false;
    16	    let old = crate::compile(target);
    17	    let next = crate::compile(current);
    18	    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    19	        return Bridge {
    20	            sources: target.to_vec(),
    21	            changes: vec![],
    22	            diagnostics: old.diagnostics,
    23	        };
    24	    }
    25	    if !next.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    26	        return Bridge {
    27	            sources: current.to_vec(),
    28	            changes: vec![],
    29	            diagnostics: next.diagnostics,
    30	        };
    31	    }
    32	    if contracts(&old, &next) {
    33	        return retained(current.to_vec(), false);
    34	    }
    35	    match alpha(target, current) {
    36	        Ok(Some(sources)) => {
    37	            let bound = crate::compile(&sources);
    38	            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    39	                return Bridge {
    40	                    sources,
    41	                    changes: vec![],
    42	                    diagnostics: bound.diagnostics,
    43	                };
    44	            }
    45	            if contracts(&old, &bound) {
    46	                return retained(sources, true);
    47	            }
    48	        }
    49	        Err(error) => {*guarded_fallback=error.unsupported;return refused(current, &error.reason)},
    50	        Ok(None) => {}
    51	    }
    52	    *guarded_fallback=true;
    53	    refused(current,"the complete current workflow does not retain every predecessor table, field, operation and accepted input")
    54	}
    55	fn retained(sources: Vec<Source>, alpha: bool) -> Bridge {
    56	    Bridge {sources,diagnostics:vec![],changes:vec![format!("current independent intent: retained the complete current authored schema, defaults, programs, helpers, jobs and policy declarations{}; predecessor names and accepted inputs remain available; review current results in the old UI",if alpha {" under declared table identity renames"}else{""})]}
    57	}
    58	fn refused(current: &[Source], reason: &str) -> Bridge {
    59	    Bridge {sources:current.to_vec(),changes:vec![],diagnostics:vec![crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),format!("rollback view cannot preserve current independent intent: {reason}"),"author a compatible guarded source retaining the predecessor fields and inputs, truthful migrations and the current policy; an acknowledgment does not authorize restoring an unsafe workflow").guide("deploy")]}
    60	}

    62	/// A loosened current workflow may omit a predecessor's nullable, default-free
    63	/// field rather than invent its value. All current schema and executable
    64	/// declarations stay intact; old wire aliases are the only source additions.
    65	pub(super) fn guarded(target:&[Source],current:&[Source])->Bridge {
    66	    let normalized=match alpha(target,current) {
    67	        Ok(sources)=>sources,
    68	        Err(error)=>return refused(current,&error.reason),
    69	    };
    70	    let current=normalized.as_deref().unwrap_or(current);
    71	    let old=crate::compile(target);let next=crate::compile(current);
    72	    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty()||!next.diagnostics.is_empty(){return refused(current,"both authored sources must compile before preparing a guarded view");}
    73	    if !table_contracts(&old,&next,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow needs every predecessor table and required/defaulted field; author a truthful reverse projection or compatible guarded source");}
    74	    let mut sources=current.to_vec();let mut changes=vec!["guarded loosened view: retained the complete current schema, defaults, helpers, programs, jobs, aliases and independent intent; omitted nullable default-free predecessor fields are absent results, never invented values; review current results in the old UI".into()];
    75	    if normalized.is_some(){changes.push("declared table identities: complete current guarded workflow retained under predecessor spellings, without rewriting business strings or weakening authority".into());}
    76	    for(file,text)in &mut sources {
    77	        let doc=parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
    78	        let Some((prior_file,prior_text))=target.iter().find(|(f,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(f,"")==super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,""))else{continue};
    79	        let prior=parser::parse_document(prior_file,prior_text).0;
    80	        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);let mut edits=Vec::new();let mut extra=String::new();let mut restored=BTreeMap::new();
    81	        // Retain a missing old exported alias only to an authored current
    82	        // endpoint (including an explicitly authored current alias target).
    83	        // Existing/rebound current aliases remain exactly current-authored.
    84	        for(name,target,_)in &prior.aliases {
    85	            let qualified=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name);
    86	            if next.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==qualified){continue;}
    87	            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target).or_else(||doc.aliases.iter().find(|(alias,_,_)|alias==target).and_then(|(_,target,_)|doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)));
    88	            if let Some(endpoint)=endpoint{extra.push_str(&format!("alias {name} = {}\n",endpoint.name));restored.insert(qualified,endpoint.name.clone());}
    89	        }
    90	        for previous in &old.programs {
    91	            let qualified=&previous.program.name;
    92	            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&p.name)==*qualified).or_else(|| {
    93	                let target=doc.aliases.iter().find(|(name,_,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name)==*qualified).map(|(_,target,_)|target).or_else(||restored.get(qualified))?;
    94	                doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)
    95	            });
    96	            let Some(endpoint)=endpoint else{continue};
    97	            let actual=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&endpoint.name);
    98	            let Some(current)=next.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*qualified||p.program.name==actual)else{continue};
    99	            for(alias,owner)in &previous.program.arg_aliases {
   100	                if current.program.argument(alias).is_some(){continue;}
   101	                let Some((canonical,_))=current.program.argument(owner)else{continue};
   102	                let Some(arg)=endpoint.args.iter().find(|arg|arg.name==canonical&&arg.alias.is_none())else{continue};
   103	                if let Ok((_,end))=arg_type(text,&tokens,arg){edits.push((end,end,format!(" alias {alias}")));changes.push(format!("operation {qualified}: retained wire alias {alias} on {canonical}"));}
   104	            }
   105	        }
   106	        edits.sort();edits.dedup();edits.sort_by_key(|edit|std::cmp::Reverse(edit.0));
   107	        for(start,end,value)in edits{text.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
   108	        if !extra.is_empty(){if !text.ends_with('\n'){text.push('\n');}text.push_str(&extra);}
   109	    }
   110	    let bound=crate::compile(&sources);
   111	    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty(){return Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:bound.diagnostics};}
   112	    if !table_contracts(&old,&bound,true)||!endpoint_contracts(&old,&bound,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow cannot retain a predecessor query or a retained export's wire aliases and distinct inputs; author a compatible guarded endpoint with the old accepted arguments");}
   113	    for(name,table)in &old.schema.tables {
   114	        for column in table.columns.keys().filter(|column|!bound.schema.tables[name].columns.contains_key(*column)) {
   115	            if let Some(current)=renamed_column(&old,&bound,name,column){changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: declared identity is now {name}.{current}; guarded view uses the current spelling and result semantics; review the old UI"));}
   116	            else{changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: removed nullable default-free field remains absent in this guarded view; historical cells stay retained; review the old UI result"));}
   117	        }
   118	    }
   119	    for previous in &old.programs {
   120	        if !bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==previous.program.name){changes.push(format!("operation {}: paused because no compatible current authored write endpoint exists; old calls return E_OP; author a guarded compatibility endpoint if this old workflow is still needed",previous.program.name));}
   121	    }
   122	    Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:vec![]}
   123	}
   124	
   125	pub(super) fn contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound)->bool {
   126	    table_contracts(old,new,false)&&endpoint_contracts(old,new,false)
   127	}
   128	fn table_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,nullable_gaps:bool)->bool {
   129	    old.schema.tables.iter().all(|(name,table)|new.schema.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|next| {
   130	        table.columns.iter().all(|(column,kind)|next.columns.contains_key(column)
   131	            || (nullable_gaps&&renamed_column(old,new,name,column).is_some())
   132	            || (nullable_gaps&&kind.is_optional()&&!table.defaults.contains_key(column)&&!table.engine_field(column)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(column)))
   133	    }))
   134	}
   135	fn endpoint_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,pause_removed_writes:bool)->bool {
   136	    old.programs.iter().all(|p|new.programs.iter().find(|n|n.program.name==p.program.name)
   137	        .map_or_else(||pause_removed_writes&&matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action),|n|endpoint_contract(&p.program,&n.program)))
   138	}
   139	pub(super) fn endpoint_contract(old:&snapback4_core::Program,new:&snapback4_core::Program)->bool {
   140	    if old.kind!=new.kind{return false;}
   141	    let mut positions=BTreeSet::new();
   142	    for(name,kind)in &old.args {
   143	        let Some((target,next))=new.argument(name)else{return false};
   144	        if !accepts(kind,next)||!positions.insert(target){return false;}
   145	        if !old.arg_aliases.iter().filter(|(_,owner)|*owner==name).all(|(alias,_)|new.argument(alias).is_some_and(|(canonical,next)|canonical==target&&accepts(kind,next))){return false;}
   146	    }
   147	    new.args.iter().all(|(name,kind)|positions.contains(name.as_str())||matches!(kind,ArgType::Optional(_)))
   148	}
   149	fn renamed_column<'a>(old:&crate::Bound,new:&'a crate::Bound,table:&str,column:&str)->Option<&'a str> {
   150	    let next=new.schema.table(table)?;
   151	    if let Some((name,_))=new.schema.asset_renames.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|(name,prior)|prior.as_str()==column&&next.columns.contains_key(*name)){return Some(name.as_str());}
   152	    if let Some(prior)=old.schema.asset_renames.get(table).and_then(|m|m.get(column)) {
   153	        if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
   154	    }
   155	    for transform in &new.schema.backfills {
   156	        if transform.table==table&&transform.inputs.len()==1&&transform.inputs.contains_key(column)&&transform.outputs.len()==1 {
   157	            let name=transform.outputs.iter().next()?;
   158	            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{column}:{name}")&&next.columns.contains_key(name){return Some(name.as_str());}
   159	        }
   160	    }
   161	    for transform in &old.schema.backfills {
   162	        if transform.table==table&&transform.outputs.len()==1&&transform.outputs.contains(column)&&transform.inputs.len()==1 {
   163	            let prior=transform.inputs.keys().next()?;
   164	            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{prior}:{column}") {
   165	                if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
   166	            }
   167	        }
   168	    }
   169	    None
   170	}
   171	// This is inclusion of wire domains for an unchanged authored current body,
   172	// not synthesis of a conversion or permission to reinterpret old arguments.
   173	fn accepts(old: &ArgType, new: &ArgType) -> bool {
   174	    if old == new {
   175	        return true;
   176	    }
   177	    match (old, new) {
   178	        (ArgType::Optional(a), ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(a, b),
   179	        (_, ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(old, b),
   180	        (ArgType::Optional(_), _) => false,
   181	        (
   182	            ArgType::Text {
   183	                min: a,
   184	                max: b,
   185	                format: f,
   186	            },
   187	            ArgType::Text {
   188	                min: x,
   189	                max: y,
   190	                format: g,
   191	            },
   192	        ) => f == g && x <= a && b <= y,
   193	        (ArgType::Enum { variants: a }, ArgType::Enum { variants: b }) => {
   194	            a.iter().all(|v| b.contains(v))
   195	        }
   196	        (
   197	            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
   198	            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
   199	        ) => {
   200	            let parts = |kind: &ArgType| match kind {
   201	                ArgType::List { of, max } => (0, *max, of.clone()),
   202	                ArgType::ListRange { of, min, max } => (*min, *max, of.clone()),
   203	                _ => unreachable!(),
   204	            };
   205	            let (a, b, element) = parts(old);
   206	            let (x, y, next) = parts(new);
   207	            x <= a
   208	                && b <= y
   209	                && match (element, next) {
   210	                    (None, None) => true,
   211	                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => accepts(&a, &b),
   212	                    _ => false,
   213	                }
   214	        }
   215	        (ArgType::IntRange { .. }, ArgType::Int) => true,
   216	        (ArgType::IntRange { min: a, max: b }, ArgType::IntRange { min: x, max: y }) => {
   217	            x <= a && b <= y
   218	        }
   219	        _ => false,
   220	    }
   221	}
   222	fn word(name: &str) -> bool {
   223	    !name.is_empty() && name.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
   224	}
   225	fn offset(text: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> usize {
   226	    let start = text
   227	        .split_inclusive('\n')
   228	        .take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
   229	        .map(str::len)
   230	        .sum::<usize>();
   231	    start
   232	        + text[start..]
   233	            .chars()
   234	            .take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
   235	            .map(char::len_utf8)
   236	            .sum::<usize>()
   237	}
   238	fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize) {
   239	    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
   240	    let end = text
   241	        .split_inclusive('\n')
   242	        .enumerate()
   243	        .skip(line as usize)
   244	        .find(|(_, s)| {
   245	            !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--")
   246	        })
   247	        .map(|(i, _)| offset(text, i as u32 + 1, 1))
   248	        .unwrap_or(text.len());
   249	    (start, end)
   250	}

    21	// Timeline event slots are compiler-owned delivery metadata, not authored old

    26	fn risks_for(candidate:&Schema,old:&Schema,release:u64,dir:&Path,include_new_tables:bool,include_constraints:bool)->Vec<String>{
    27	    let names:std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>=candidate.table_renames.iter().filter(|(new,prior)|old.table(prior).is_some() && old.table(new).is_none()).map(|(new,prior)|(prior.clone(),new.clone())).collect();
    28	    if names.is_empty(){return risks_aligned(candidate,old,release,dir,include_new_tables,include_constraints)}
    29	    let aligned=match old.with_table_names(&names){Ok(schema)=>schema,Err(error)=>return vec![format!("rollback foresight cannot compare renamed identities: {error}; run exact rollback --check")]};
    30	    risks_aligned(candidate,&aligned,release,dir,include_new_tables,include_constraints).into_iter().map(|line|{
    31	        let (mut facts,command)=line.split_once("; run `").map_or((line.clone(),String::new()),|(facts,command)|(facts.to_owned(),format!("; run `{command}")));
    32	        for (prior,new) in &names {facts=facts.replace(&format!(", {new}."),&format!(", {prior} (current {new}).")).replace(&format!("table {new}:"),&format!("table {prior} (current {new}):")).replace(&format!("unique index {new}."),&format!("unique index {prior} (current {new})."));}
    33	        facts+&command
    34	    }).collect()
    35	}
    36	fn risks_aligned(candidate:&Schema,old:&Schema,release:u64,dir:&Path,include_new_tables:bool,include_constraints:bool)->Vec<String>{
    37	    let mut risks:Vec<_>=successor::classify(candidate,old).into_iter().filter_map(|step| {
    38	        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.")?;
    39	        let (table,column)=path.split_once(".columns.")?;
    40	        let target=old.tables.get(table)?;
    41	        if target.engine_field(column) || snapback4_core::schema::timeline::is_slot(column) || old.maintain_for(table).is_some() || target.maintained.contains_key(column) {return None;}
    42	        let before=target.columns.get(column)?;
    43	        if projected(candidate,table,column,before) {return None;}
    44	
    45	        let from=candidate.tables.get(table)?;
    46	        let source_kind=from.columns.get(column).or_else(||candidate.asset_renames.get(table)?.iter().find(|(_,old)|old.as_str()==column).and_then(|(new,_)|from.columns.get(new)));
    47	        let reason=match source_kind {
    48	            None if !before.is_optional() && !matches!(target.defaults.get(column),Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(_))) =>
    49	                "without a fitting reverse projection, new rows will lack the old required field, so rollback can refuse".to_owned(),
    50	            Some(kind) if kind!=before && matches!(step.change,Change::Narrowing|Change::Destructive) =>
    51	                format!("new writes allowed by {kind} may not fit {before}, so rollback can hide rows; {}", remedy(before)),
    52	            _ => return None,
    53	        };
    54	        Some(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, {table}.{column}: {reason}; keep an expand/contract bridge with the old required fields and compatible domains; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()))
    55	    }).collect();
    56	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#19-dormant-visibility-and-migration-dorm — warn before new writes, including harmless newer-only facts and empty legacy cells.
    57	    for (name, _) in candidate.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none() && candidate.maintain_for(name).is_none()) {
    58	        if include_new_tables && old.table(name).is_none() && !candidate.table_renames.get(name).is_some_and(|source|old.table(source).is_some()) {
    59	            risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: rows written to new table {name} will be hidden while release {release} is live; release {release} has no such table, so these are newer-only facts, not lost old-table records; a same-table `down` cannot create this missing table; keep it in a loosened bridge or declare a table rename; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    60	        }
    61	    }
    62	    for (name,target) in old.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
    63	        let Some(from)=candidate.tables.get(name) else {continue;};
    64	        if old.maintain_for(name).is_some() {continue;}
    65	        for (column,kind) in &target.columns {
    66	            if projected(candidate,name,column,kind) || from.columns.contains_key(column) || target.engine_field(column) || snapback4_core::schema::timeline::is_slot(column) || target.maintained.contains_key(column) {continue;}
    67	            let interpretation=if kind.is_optional() {"null".to_owned()} else if let Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value))=target.defaults.get(column) {format!("the old default {}",snapback4_core::wire::to_json(value))} else {continue;};
    68	            let suggestions=cross_table_suggestions(candidate,old,name,column,kind,target.defaults.get(column));
    69	            risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, {name}.{column}: without a fitting reverse projection, records created while this field is absent have no retained old cell and will show {interpretation} to release {release} users; declare a reverse projection from the replacement value, or retain the field in a bridge release; {suggestions}run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    70	        }
    71	        // Registration compares constraint domains structurally in
    72	        // release_bridge::requires_view (including renamed fields). General
    73	        // foresight advice also explains constraint changes, but is not a
    74	        // second, less precise admission predicate.
    75	        if include_constraints {
    76	        for (index,definition) in &target.indexes {
    77	            if definition.unique && from.indexes.get(index)!=Some(definition) {
    78	                risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, unique index {name}.{index}: future writes may introduce duplicate keys and refuse restoration; keep the old uniqueness in a bridge; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    79	            }
    80	        }
    81	        if target.constraints.iter().any(|constraint| {
    82	            if from.constraints.contains(constraint){return false;}
    83	            match constraint {
    84	                snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field,..}=>!target.columns.get(field).is_some_and(|kind|projected(candidate,name,field,kind)),
    85	                snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Cap{by,max}=>!from.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Cap{by:next_by,max:next_max}if next_by==by&&next_max<=max)),
    86	                snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{..}=>true,
    87	                _=>false,
    88	            }
    89	        }) {
    90	            risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, table {name}: changed constraints may let future writes refuse rollback; preserve old bounds/quotas in a bridge; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    91	        }
    92	        }
    93	    }
    94	    risks
    95	}
    96	
    97	fn projected(candidate:&Schema,table:&str,column:&str,kind:&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType)->bool {
    98	    candidate.extractions.iter().any(|e|e.parent==table&&e.fields.values().any(|c|c==column)) || (scalar(kind) && candidate.downfills.iter().any(|f|f.table==table && f.outputs.contains(column))) || candidate.asset_renames.get(table).is_some_and(|map|map.iter().any(|(new,old)|old==column && candidate.tables.get(table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(new))==Some(kind)))
    99	}
   100	fn scalar(kind:&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType)->bool {
   101	    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::*;
   102	    match kind {Optional(k)=>scalar(k),Image{..}|Video{..}|File{..}|List{..}|ListRange{..}|Ref{..}=>false,_=>true}
   103	}
   104	fn remedy(kind:&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType)->&'static str {
   105	    if scalar(kind) {"declare a truthful scalar `down`, or generate a loosened bridge"}
   106	    else {"computed `down` cannot project files, images, collections or references; generate a loosened bridge, or keep the required value and add a status"}
   107	}

   110	mod tests {
   111	    use super::*;
   112	    fn schema(source:&str)->Schema {
   113	        let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
   114	        assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);b.schema
   115	    }
   116	    #[test]
   117	    fn warns_before_any_future_rows_exist_but_compatible_additions_are_quiet() {
   118	        let base="table items:\n  required: int 0..10\n  state: enum('old')\n  public\n";
   119	        let old=schema(base);
   120	        for (candidate,subject) in [
   121	            (base.replace("  required: int 0..10\n",""),"items.required"),
   122	            (base.replace("int 0..10","int 0..20"),"items.required"),
   123	            (base.replace("enum('old')","enum('old','new')"),"items.state"),
   124	            (base.replace("int 0..10","int 0..10 ?"),"items.required"),
   125	        ] {
   126	            let warnings=risks(&schema(&candidate),&old,1,Path::new("deployment"));
   127	            assert!(warnings.iter().any(|w|w.contains(subject)&&w.contains("--to 1 --check --bridge")),"{warnings:?}");
   128	        }
   129	        assert!(risks(&schema(&base.replace("  public","  label: text <=20 ?\n  public")),&old,1,Path::new("deployment")).is_empty());
   130	        let unique=schema(&base.replace("  public","  unique state\n  public"));
   131	        assert!(risks(&old,&unique,1,Path::new("deployment")).iter().any(|w|w.contains("duplicate keys")));
   132	    }
   133	    #[test]

   136	        let next=schema("table items:\n  public\ntable explanations:\n  text: text <=40\n  public\n");
   137	        let warnings=risks(&next,&old,1,Path::new("deployment"));

   139	        assert!(warnings.iter().any(|w|w.contains("items.reason") && w.contains("show null") && w.contains("reverse projection")),"{warnings:?}");
   140	    }
   141	    #[test]
   142	    fn declared_down_and_scalar_or_asset_renames_do_not_warn_about_absent_fields() {
   143	        let old=schema("table items:\n  state: enum('old')\n  public\n");
   144	        let new=schema("table items:\n  state: enum('old','new')\n  public\ndown items oldState(state: enum('old','new')): {state:'old'}\n");
   145	        assert!(risks(&new,&old,1,Path::new("deployment")).is_empty());
   146	        for kind in ["text <=40 ?","file <=1024 ?"] {
   147	            let old=schema(&format!("table items:\n  title: {kind}\n  public\n"));
   148	            let new=schema(&format!("table items:\n  name: {kind} renamed from title\n  public\n"));
   149	            assert!(risks(&new,&old,1,Path::new("deployment")).is_empty(),"{kind}");
   150	        }
   151	    }

   159	        assert!(!warnings.iter().any(|w|w.contains("new table records")),"{warnings:?}");

   163	
   164	fn cross_table_suggestions(candidate:&Schema,old:&Schema,parent:&str,column:&str,kind:&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType,default:Option<&ColumnDefault>)->String {
   165	    let fallback=match default {Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(v))=>match v{snapback4_core::Value::Bool(v)=>v.to_string(),snapback4_core::Value::Int(v)=>v.to_string(),snapback4_core::Value::String(v)=>snapback4_lang::print::quote(v),_=>return String::new()},_ if kind.is_optional()=>"null".into(),_=>return String::new()};
   166	    let mut proposals=Vec::new();
   167	    for(child,table)in candidate.visible_tables().filter(|(name,_)|old.table(name).is_none()) {
   168	        for(reference,ty)in &table.columns {
   169	            if !matches!(ty.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref{table} if table==parent){continue}
   170	            let values=table.columns.iter().filter(|(c,k)|!table.engine_field(c)&&c.as_str()!=reference.as_str()&&k.required()==kind.required()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   171	            let unique=table.indexes.values().any(|i|i.unique&&i.columns()==vec![reference.clone()]);
   172	            if unique&&values.len()==1 {
   173	                let value=values[0].0;
   174	                proposals.push(format!("candidate cross-table down (confirm the replacement field's meaning): `down {parent} rollback{column}(id: id): {{{column}: {child}[{reference} = id].{value} ?? {fallback}}}`"));
   175	            }else if kind.required()==&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Bool {
   176	                proposals.push(format!("if child existence exactly means {column}, candidate cross-table down: `down {parent} rollback{column}(id: id): {{{column}: exists {child} where .{reference} = id}}`"));
   177	            }
   178	        }
   179	    }
   180	    if proposals.is_empty(){String::new()}else{proposals.join("; ")+"; "}
   181	}

    64	def witness(h, pg_url):
    65	    work, cli, start, stop, http = (h[k] for k in ('WORK','cli','start','stop','http'))
    66	    transcripts = {}
    67	    for backend, target in [('postgres', pg_url), ('sqlite', None)]:
    68	        deployment = work / ('ev1-' + backend)
    69	        records = []
    70	        assert V3 != V1 and 'format url ?' in V3

    72	            (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(source)
    73	            args = ['deploy', str(deployment)]
    74	            if version == 3: args += ['--restore-dormant']
    75	            cli(args, url=target)
    76	            child, base = start('ev1-'+backend+'-v'+str(version),target,deployment=deployment)
    77	            guest = http(base,'POST','/auth/guest',{})['session']
    78	            token = guest['token']
    79	            state = http(base,'GET','/schema',token=token)
    80	            assert state['generation']==version,state
    81	            def capture(label, value):
    82	                # Each backend mints its own guest principal. Check its value
    83	                # before excluding that nondeterministic identity from comparison.
    84	                if ' q ' in label: assert value['viewer']==guest['principal'],value
    85	                if 'store_id' in value: assert value['store_id']==state['store_id'],value
    86	                independent = {k:v for k,v in value.items() if k not in ('store_id','now','viewer')}
    87	                records.append((label,json.dumps(independent,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))))
    88	                return value
    89	            def mutate(name, identity, args):
    90	                return capture(f'v{version} m {name}',http(base,'POST','/m/'+name,{'id':identity,'args':args,'store_id':state['store_id']},token))
    91	            def query(name, args):
    92	                return capture(f'v{version} q {name}',http(base,'POST','/q/'+name,{'args':args},token))
    93	            if version == 1:
    94	                assert mutate('put','ev1-a',{'key':'a','n':2,'memo':'雪\0exact','site':'https://example.com'})['state']=='sent'

    96	                assert mutate('archive','ev1-z',{'key':'z','text':'retained row'})['state']=='sent'
    97	                first = query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']
    98	                archived = query('archived',{'key':'z'})['data']
    99	                assert [r['n'] for r in query('ordered',{})['data']]==[1,2]
   100	            elif version == 2:
   101	                changed = query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']

    31	pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &mut Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    32	    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.extractions) {
    33	        let extraction=snapback4_core::schema::Extraction{child:d.child.clone(),parent:d.parent.clone(),reference:d.reference.clone(),fields:d.fields.iter().cloned().collect()};
    34	        schema.extractions.push(extraction);
    35	    }
    36	    let mut forward_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut forward_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    37	    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.backfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);}
    38	    let mut down_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut down_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    39	    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.downfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);}
    40	    for table in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
    41	        if let Some(old)=&table.renamed_from {
    42	            if old==&table.name || catalog.tables.contains_key(old) || schema.table_renames.values().any(|v|v==old) {
    43	                diagnostics.push(type_error(&table.site,"table rename requires one distinct predecessor-only source","keep one name for each row lifetime"));
    44	            } else {schema.table_renames.insert(table.name.clone(),old.clone());}
    45	        }
    46	        for column in table.columns.iter().filter(|c|c.renamed_from.is_some()) {
    47	            let old=column.renamed_from.as_ref().unwrap();
    48	            if old==&column.name || table.columns.iter().any(|c|&c.name==old) {
    49	                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("rename source {}.{old} must be a distinct predecessor-only column",table.name),"remove the current old column and retain only the renamed declaration"));continue
    50	            }
    51	            fn asset(kind:&ast::TypeKind)->bool {match kind {ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}=>true,ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>asset(&item.kind),_=>false}}
    52	            if (asset(&column.type_.kind)||matches!(&column.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref{..})) && column.maintained.is_none() && column.sequence.is_none() && column.written_by.is_none() {

bindings, read rules, payload guards and field ownership. The compiled bound IR
must equal current authority after declared identity alignment. A release switch
is a view over the same current authoritative rows, never restoration of a
historical database snapshot: deleted rows stay deleted and current values
stay current except declared truthful migrations. No resurrection or replay of
historical preimages over authored current images is authorized. With no
hiding and no domain enlarging beyond current, those row populations remain
the current populations; changing read visibility still follows exact current
authority.
For intentional hiding, IR equality alone is insufficient: §20.2 additionally
checks population-sensitive authority/guards under the actual writer. Removed old immutability/uniqueness
constraints follow explicitly relaxed current constraints. Newer-only tables
retain current rules without promising old clients expose new concepts. Native

Names and accepted input contracts remain preserved. Dormant restoration advice

numeric view id, executable digest, changed columns, carried guards and hiding

control digest includes its metadata. Prepared views are usable only after the

#### Current independent intent and authored execution closure — Integration 23

A generated view must not restore a predecessor workflow that violates current
independent intent. Copying invariant declarations, table rules or `require`
statements alone is insufficient: a changed literal, helper, grant mutation,
write key, default, control path, returned value or system/time dependency can
change the reachable effect without changing any copied guard. This applies
both to newly declared intent and intent already present in the predecessor.
Finite challenge campaigns are regression evidence, not an equivalence proof.

The presence of an invariant never makes ordinary schema evolution require a
view. The wave-25 replay exposed the Integration 23 regression in the earlier
"any invariant plus changed code" rule: it falsely refused ten of fifty v2
deploys, including simple renames and a split with a typed down. View registration
uses structural widening and old-row foresight facts, including hiding and
misrepresentation, rather than a general executable-digest change.

Final-existence obligations compare compiled constraints under injective declared
table and column identities, including typed reference keys and ordered plain-index
columns. Index labels and source spelling are not semantic changes; missing or
changed obligations still trigger registration. Literal values remain literal.

There is one repair protection independent of row-domain widening: behind
unchanged authored data/intent declarations and executable signatures, a changed
implementation with declared intent prepares a view. Signatures include module
identity and operation wire contracts; function/program bodies are excluded.
Thus a literal/helper-only policy repair or an executable-only worker/native
repair cannot restore the older executable behind unchanged intent. Identical
executable content remains a no-op; ordinary data/intent/contract evolution is
subject to the visibility trigger instead. Compiler-owned timeline event slots
are delivery metadata, not authored predecessor cells; retiring a rebuildable
feed alone does not require a rollback view. Ordinary authored fields in those
same source tables remain subject to the complete visibility check.

Preparation first attempts the **complete current authored source closure**,
including every declaration, schema domain, default, helper, program, job and
invariant. That strategy requires every predecessor table/column spelling and
operation name/kind and accepts every old argument under its wire name (including
exported aliases). An explicitly authored parameter alias may retain an old wire
name after a canonical rename. Every old spelling for one position must resolve
to the same current position, and distinct old positions must remain distinct.
Current result semantics remain a disclosed RESULT change. The same complete
closure is also a fallback for a guard/helper transport failure without intent.

If the strict complete-closure contract is unavailable, an intent-bearing view
uses a **guarded current workflow**. Its schema, defaults, helpers, programs,
shapes, jobs, aliases and independent intent remain the current authored source;
it never copies an old executable body or helper. A removed nullable,
default-free, non-engine, non-maintained predecessor field may remain absent in
this view instead of fabricating its value. The view reports that result change;
the old cell stays retained, and an old UI may need its usual missing-field
fallback. Required/defaulted predecessor fields and every predecessor table
still need a truthful compatible representation. A declared scalar or asset/reference
column identity rename may use its current spelling and current result semantics,
with an explicit old-UI result-change disclosure; this uses the compiler
validated identity mapping rather than inventing a predecessor value. The
same closed, capture-checked table alpha adapter may run before this
guarded-current strategy, so a nullable field gap does not discard a valid
declared table identity. Its current authority is compared under those identities
before strategy selection; an unsafe or unsupported alpha mapping still refuses.
No legacy schema field is introduced into current expression/name resolution.

Every predecessor query and every retained export must preserve its old accepted
wire inputs, aliases and distinct input positions. An unused parameter-alias slot
may retain an old spelling on the same current canonical argument; collisions,
rebinding, narrowing and unsupported mappings refuse. A missing old exported
alias may point only at an already authored compatible current endpoint.
Current explicit/rebound aliases retain their exact current target and guards.
A genuinely removed mutation, job or action may instead pause: its absence and
`E_OP` are named, and the author can supply a compatible current guarded endpoint
if that old workflow is still needed. Old code is never revived to make a removed
write available. Queries remain required so a view does not silently discard the
predecessor read surface. Writer/CAS, continuation and other activation gates
continue to apply to both strategies.

Without independent intent, the original selective guarded bridge remains
available. Its dependency checks resolve qualified helper calls to the same
validated global helper identity as the compiler, including transitive calls. Helper parameter types and row-reference AST slots
are compared under forward/inverse declared table identities; business strings,
member spellings and helper identities are never token-replaced.
A replaced or rebound exported alias or untransportable guard/dependency falls back to the
current workflow strategies above. Mixed-domain fallback uses the same compiled
qualified endpoint/alias contract; there is no unqualified source-text shortcut.
The selective predecessor workflow keeps its own accepted old wire contract;
current aliases cannot merge or rebind distinct old input positions behind
transported current guards. The complete-current and guarded-current strategies
above instead require the compiled current endpoint to accept every old input.
Guarded exports retain complete statement
order, query filters, helper calls and declared row identities; a differing ordered body or named-shape dependency
selects the current workflow instead of reconstructing authorization from the
last textual binding alone. Execution comparisons retain the complete AST,
including every merge lane prefix, source, loop domain, filter and projection;
only diagnostic source locations and validated row-identity spellings are
normalized. Human-readable expression printing is not a semantic signature.
Unrelated schema/domain additions are not guard
dependencies, and declared table identities are compared under their typed
renames rather than by raw spelling. Named shape definitions (including transitive
spreads) belong to that execution context; unchanged shape-use text does not
establish unchanged guard meaning. Field-authority mismatches participate in
strategy selection before the CLI admission gate, so a truthful guarded-current
view can be selected instead of refusing a valid deployment. Current shared-column defaults and authority are
checked after compilation.
A failed/capturing/ambiguous typed identity mapping cannot authorize fallback.
Source edits use original UTF-8 spans bounded by original file length even
without a final LF. Quota relaxation is structural registration input; tightening
on the same grouping is not. General constraint advice is not a second, less
precise registration predicate. Refusals identify the compatible guarded endpoint,
wire alias or truthful reverse projection to author. A hiding acknowledgment
never authorizes restoring an unsafe workflow, and ordinary security findings,
gates and runtime invariant boundaries still apply.

     8	fn table(table:&mut Table,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){
     9	    table.columns=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.columns),map);
    10	    table.defaults=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.defaults),map);
    11	    table.column_reads=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.column_reads),map);
    12	    table.on_delete=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.on_delete),map);
    13	    table.written_by=replace_keys(std::mem::take(&mut table.written_by),map);

    15	    fn rule(r:&mut crate::schema::RuleExpr,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>){use crate::schema::RuleExpr::*;match r {Eq(a,b)|Ne(a,b)|Compare(_,a,b)=>{term(a,map);term(b,map)},And(xs)|Or(xs)=>for x in xs{rule(x,map)},Not(r)=>rule(r,map),Exists{key,equalities,..}|Created{key,equalities,..}=>{for t in key{term(t,map)}for(_,t)in equalities{term(t,map)}},_=>()}}
    16	    for r in [&mut table.rules.read,&mut table.rules.insert,&mut table.rules.update,&mut table.rules.delete]{rule(r,map)}

    35	pub(super) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,maps:&Maps)->Schema {
    36	    for(name,map)in maps {if let Some(t)=schema.tables.get_mut(name){table(t,map);}}
    37	    for e in &mut schema.extractions {
    38	        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.child){e.reference=map.get(&e.reference).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||e.reference.clone());e.fields=std::mem::take(&mut e.fields).into_iter().map(|(dest,source)|(map.get(&dest).cloned().unwrap_or(dest),source)).collect();}
    39	        if let Some(map)=maps.get(&e.parent){for source in e.fields.values_mut(){if let Some(new)=map.get(source){*source=new.clone();}}}
    40	    }schema
    41	}
    42	pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&BTreeMap<String,i64>,renamed_tables:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Maps,RuntimeError>{
    43	    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_asset_column_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    44	    if !exists && next.asset_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(Maps::new())}
    45	    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _asset_column_renames(t INTEGER NOT NULL,name TEXT NOT NULL,origin TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,name)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    46	    let mut maps=Maps::new();
    47	    for (name,current) in &next.tables {
    48	        let Some(t)=catalog.get(name) else {continue};
    49	        let mut aliases:BTreeMap<String,String>=conn.prepare("SELECT name,origin FROM _asset_column_renames WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    50	        for (new,old) in next.asset_renames.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
    51	            if old==new || current.columns.contains_key(old) || !current.columns.get(new).is_some_and(asset){return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} requires one predecessor-only source and one asset destination")))}
    52	            let root=aliases.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
    53	            if aliases.get(new).is_some_and(|r|r!=&root){return Err(refusal("asset rename cannot merge independent column lifetimes"))}
    54	            for c in [old,new] {aliases.insert(c.clone(),root.clone());conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _asset_column_renames VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,c,root])?;}
    55	        }
    56	        let mut selected=BTreeMap::new();for c in current.columns.keys(){if let Some(root)=aliases.get(c){if selected.insert(root.clone(),c.clone()).is_some(){return Err(refusal("two names for the same asset column cannot be active together"))}}}
    57	        let map:BTreeMap<_,_>=aliases.into_iter().filter_map(|(old,root)|selected.get(&root).filter(|new|**new!=old).map(|new|(old,new.clone()))).collect();
    58	        for(old,new)in &map {
    59	            let prior:Option<String>=conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
    60	            if let Some(prior)=prior {
    61	                let prior:Schema=serde_json::from_str(&prior).map_err(|e|refusal(e.to_string()))?;
    62	                let prior=prior.with_table_names(renamed_tables)?;
    63	                if let Some(before)=prior.table(name).filter(|t|t.columns.contains_key(old)){
    64	                    let source=&before.columns[old];let destination=&current.columns[new];
    65	                    if reference(source)||reference(destination){
    66	                        if source!=destination||before.on_delete.get(old)!=current.on_delete.get(new)||before.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*old)||current.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.reference==*new){return Err(refusal("reference rename requires identical nominal type, deletion policy and uncaptured ownership in both directions"))}
    67	                    }
    68	                }
    69	            }
    70	            for rows in ["r","_dormant_rows","_dormant_narrow_rows"] {
    71	                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
    72	                let a=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(old).unwrap());let b=format!("$.Row.{}",serde_json::to_string(new).unwrap());
    73	                let conflict:bool=conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {rows} WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL AND json_type(data,?3) IS NOT NULL AND json_extract(data,?2)!=json_extract(data,?3))"),params![t,a,b],|r|r.get(0))?;
    74	                if conflict{return Err(refusal(format!("asset rename {name}.{old} → {new} has a conflicting physical destination")))}
    75	                conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET data=json_remove(json_set(data,?3,json_extract(data,?2)),?2) WHERE t=?1 AND json_type(data,?2) IS NOT NULL"),params![t,a,b])?;
    76	            }
    77	            conn.execute("UPDATE _assets SET home_column=?3 WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_column=?2",params![name,old,new])?;
    78	            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_refs")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_refs SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
    79	            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_values")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
    80	            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_columns")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_columns SET column_name=?3 WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![name,old,new])?;}
    81	        }
    82	        if !map.is_empty(){
    83	            for rows in ["_dormant_narrow_rows","_dormant_tables"] {
    84	                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
    85	                let condition=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{"table_name=?1"}else{"t=?1"};
    86	                let value=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{rusqlite::types::Value::Text(name.clone())}else{rusqlite::types::Value::Integer(*t)};
    87	                let definitions:Vec<String>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT DISTINCT definition FROM {rows} WHERE {condition}"))?.query_map([&value],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    88	                for text in definitions {let mut definition:Table=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;table(&mut definition,&map);conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET definition=?2 WHERE {condition} AND definition=?3"),params![value,canonical_json(&definition),text])?;}
    89	            }
    90	            maps.insert(name.clone(),map);
    91	        }
    92	    }
    93	    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[super::table_renames::VERSION])?;
    94	    Ok(maps)
    95	}
    96	fn exists_table(conn:&Connection,name:&str)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get(0))?)}

    28	fn scalar_key_rename_retains_an_explicit_legacy_lookup_on_default_rollback() {
    29	    let app=App::new();
    30	    let old="table items:\n  code: text <=20\n  n: int\n  unique byCode: code\n  public\n  online only\nquery legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code] by byCode\n";
    31	    app.source(old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    32	    app.seed(old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":"a"})]);
    33	    let current=old.replace("  code: text <=20", "  label: text <=20 renamed from code")
    34	        .replace("unique byCode: code","unique byCurrentCode: label").replace("n: int", "n: int ?")
    35	        .replace("query legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code] by byCode\n", "");
    36	    app.source(&current);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    37	    let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
    38	    assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    39	    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    40	    assert_eq!(app.json(&["sql","SELECT code FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"])["rows"][0][0],"a");
    41	    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
    42	    assert_eq!(app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"])["rows"][0][0],"a");

    76	fn scalar_renamed_unique_keys_keep_their_identity_in_default_rollback() {

    78	        for strengthen in [false,true] {
    79	        let app=App::new();
    80	        let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int\n  code: {kind}\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items first 10 by byN\n");
    81	        let old=if strengthen {old.replace("  by byN: n, id", "  by byN: n, id\n  by byCustomer: code")} else {old};
    82	        app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    83	        app.seed(&old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":value,"memo":null})]);
    84	        let next=format!("table items:\n  n: int ?\n  label: {kind} renamed from code\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, label, id\n  unique byCustomer: label\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n");
    85	        app.source(&next);
    86	        app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    87	        let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
    88	        assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    89	        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);

    91	        assert_eq!(data["rows"][0][0],value);
    92	        assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    93	        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
    94	        let forward=app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
    95	        assert_eq!(forward["rows"][0][0],value);
    96	        }
    97	    }

   420	fn invariant_apps_keep_ordinary_rename_split_and_required_field_upgrades() {
   421	    // Small independent shapes from wave-25 002/004/020/024/036/044.
   422	    // Real row images, an uploaded image and default rollback exercise storage,
   423	    // rather than relying on a successful check of empty source alone.
   424	    let base="table items:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  label: text 1..100\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nquery all():\n  return items first 10 by .id\nmutation create(label: text 1..100):\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,label}\ninvariant items.read only .owner = viewer\n";
   425	    let rename=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  title: text 1..100 renamed from label read <- .owner = viewer")
   426	        .replace("create(label: text 1..100)","create(title: text 1..100 alias label)")
   427	        .replace("{owner:viewer,label}","{owner:viewer,title}")
   428	        +"invariant items.title read only .owner = viewer\n";
   429	    let split=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  invitation: text 1..100\n  envelope: text 1..100")
   430	        .replace("create(label: text 1..100)","create(invitation: text 1..100, envelope: text 1..100)")
   431	        .replace("{owner:viewer,label}","{owner:viewer,invitation,envelope}")
   432	        +"backfill items invitationFromLabel(label: text 1..100): {invitation:label}\nbackfill items envelopeFromLabel(label: text 1..100): {envelope:label}\ndown items oldLabel(invitation: text 1..100): {label:invitation}\ninvariant items.invitation read only .owner = viewer\n";
   433	    let reference=base.replace("table items:","table records renamed from items:")
   434	        .replace("return items","return records").replace("insert items","insert records")
   435	        .replace("invariant items.","invariant records.")
   436	        +"table attachments:\n  parent: records\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\n";
   437	    let asset_old=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  label: text 1..100\n  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4")
   438	        .replace("mutation create(label: text 1..100):\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,label}\n","");
   439	    let asset_split=asset_old.replace("  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4\n","")
   440	        +"table surveys:\n  parent: items\n  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\nextract surveys from items via parent: {photos:photos}\n";
   441	    let alias=reference.replace("mutation create(label:","mutation openRecord(title:")
   442	        .replace("text 1..100):\n  return insert records {owner:viewer,label}","text 1..100 alias label):\n  return insert records {owner:viewer,label:title}")
   443	        +"alias create = openRecord\n";
   444	    let required=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  label: text 1..100\n  priority: int 1..9 default 3");
   445	    for(name,old,next,row)in [
   446	        ("column rename",base.to_owned(),rename,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
   447	        ("split with truthful down",base.to_owned(),split,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
   448	        ("nominal rename and child reference",base.to_owned(),reference,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
   449	        ("required image to child",asset_old,asset_split,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original","photos":"fixture-image"})),
   450	        ("table operation and parameter aliases",base.to_owned(),alias,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
   451	        ("add required with authored default",base.to_owned(),required,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
   452	    ] {
   453	        let app=App::new();app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);app.seed(&old,vec![row]);
   454	        app.source(&next);
   455	        let deployed=app.run(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
   456	        assert!(deployed.status.success(),"{name}: {}",text(&deployed));

   464	        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);

   469	fn pre_view_release_refusal_names_exact_selection_and_registration_upgrade() {
   470	    let app=App::new();app.source(OLD);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
   471	    let next=newer();app.source(&next);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
   472	    app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"pending","amount":null})]);
   473	    // A pre-0.4.39 parent has no registration manifest. Its immutable original

   480	        assert!(text(&refused).contains("redeploy with 0.4.39 to register a view"),"{}",text(&refused));
   481	        assert_eq!(app.count(),1,"refusal must retain the active population");
   482	    }
   483	    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
   484	    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);

   488	fn incomplete_current_shape_uses_a_guarded_bridge_with_current_intent() {
   489	    let app=App::new();
   490	    let old=OLD.replace("  amount: int 100..50000","  legacy: text <=20 ?\n  amount: int 100..50000")+"invariant items.read only true\n";
   491	    app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
   492	    let next=old.replace("  legacy: text <=20 ?\n","").replace("amount: int 100..50000","amount: int 100..50000 ?")
   493	        .replace("  return insert payments","  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT\n  return insert payments");
   494	    app.source(&next);let preview=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--check","--json"]);
   495	    assert_eq!(preview["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(),1);
   496	    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
   497	    app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"pending","amount":null})]);
   498	    let selected=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--json"])["release"].as_u64().unwrap();
   499	    assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
   500	    let root=app.0.join(format!("deployment/releases/{selected}/snapback"));
   501	    let sources=fs::read_dir(root).unwrap().map(|entry|{let p=entry.unwrap().path();(p.file_name().unwrap().to_str().unwrap().to_owned(),fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap())}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   502	    let compiled=snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);assert!(compiled.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",compiled.diagnostics);
   503	    assert_eq!(compiled.invariants.len(),1);
   504	    assert!(sources.iter().any(|(_,s)|s.contains("NO_AMOUNT")));
   505	    let store=snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&app.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
   506	    let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(store,compiled.schema,compiled.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),selected,Some(selected)).unwrap();
   507	    let out=engine.mutate("pay",None,snapback4_core::Context::new("alice",std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("pending".into()))]))).unwrap();
   508	    assert!(matches!(out.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}),"{out:?}");
   509	    assert_eq!(engine.store().count("payments").unwrap(),0);
   510	}

     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — spelling-only changes must not trigger a view.

     7	#[derive(Default)]
     8	struct Identities {
     9	    tables: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    10	    columns: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
    11	}
    12	impl Identities {
    13	    fn table(&self, name: &str) -> String {self.tables.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())}
    14	    fn column(&self, table: &str, name: &str) -> String {
    15	        self.columns.get(table).and_then(|c|c.get(name)).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())
    16	    }
    17	    fn term(&self, source: &str, term: &RuleTerm) -> RuleTerm {
    18	        match term {
    19	            RuleTerm::Field(c)=>RuleTerm::Field(self.column(source,c)),
    20	            RuleTerm::OldField(c)=>RuleTerm::OldField(self.column(source,c)),
    21	            RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>RuleTerm::NextField(self.column(source,c)),
    22	            RuleTerm::Reference{table,column,field,old}=>RuleTerm::Reference{
    23	                table:self.table(table),column:self.column(source,column),field:self.column(table,field),old:*old},
    24	            RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,hops,old}=>RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{
    25	                column:self.column(source,column),old:*old,hops:hops.iter().map(|h|snapback4_core::schema::RuleHop{
    26	                    table:self.table(&h.table),field:self.column(&h.table,&h.field)}).collect()},
    27	            RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)=>term.clone(),
    28	        }
    29	    }
    30	    fn signature(&self,schema:&Schema,source:&str,constraint:&Constraint)->Option<(String,Vec<String>,Vec<RuleTerm>)> {
    31	        let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=constraint else{return None};
    32	        let index=schema.table(table)?.index(index)?;
    33	        if !index.is_plain(){return None;}
    34	        Some((self.table(table),index.columns().iter().map(|c|self.column(table,c)).collect(),
    35	            key.iter().map(|t|self.term(source,t)).collect()))
    36	    }
    37	}
