    39	pub(super) fn relaxed(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    40	    let old=target.iter().flat_map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    41	    if !old.iter().any(|t|!t.final_exists.is_empty()){return false;}
    42	    let next=current.iter().flat_map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    43	    let (before,after)=(crate::compile(target),crate::compile(current));
    44	    if !before.diagnostics.is_empty()||!after.diagnostics.is_empty(){return true;}
    45	    let mut identities=Identities::default();
    46	    let mut pairs=Vec::new();
    47	    for prior in &old {
    48	        let Some(now)=next.iter().find(|n|n.name==prior.name||n.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&prior.name)||prior.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&n.name))else{continue};
    49	        identities.tables.insert(now.name.clone(),prior.name.clone());
    50	        let mut columns=BTreeMap::new();
    51	        for c in &prior.columns {
    52	            if let Some(n)=now.columns.iter().find(|n|n.name==c.name||n.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&c.name)||c.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&n.name)) {
    53	                columns.insert(n.name.clone(),c.name.clone());
    54	            }
    55	        }
    56	        if now.columns.iter().map(|c|columns.get(&c.name).unwrap_or(&c.name)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=now.columns.len(){return true;}
    57	        identities.columns.insert(now.name.clone(),columns);
    58	        pairs.push((prior.name.as_str(),now.name.as_str()));
    59	    }
    60	    // Never let two independent current identities collapse to one old name.
    61	    if next.iter().map(|t|identities.table(&t.name)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=next.len(){return true;}
    62	    let unchanged=Identities::default();
    63	    for (prior,now) in pairs {
    64	        let (Some(old_table),Some(new_table))=(before.schema.table(prior),after.schema.table(now))else{return true};
    65	        for constraint in &old_table.constraints {
    66	            if !matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{..}){continue;}
    67	            let Some(expected)=unchanged.signature(&before.schema,prior,constraint)else{return true};
    68	            if !new_table.constraints.iter().any(|c|identities.signature(&after.schema,now,c).as_ref()==Some(&expected)){return true;}
    69	        }
    70	    }
    71	    false
    72	}

    98	pub fn loosen(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->Bridge {
    99	    if !crate::compile(current).invariants.is_empty() {
   100	        let mut allow_guarded=false;
   101	        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut allow_guarded);
   102	        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty() || !allow_guarded{return complete;}
   103	        // A complete current closure is preferred, but a selective guarded
   104	        // predecessor is also truthful when its retained contracts compile.
   105	        let guarded=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
   106	        if guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){return guarded;}
   107	        return Bridge{diagnostics:complete.diagnostics.into_iter().chain(guarded.diagnostics).collect(),..guarded};
   108	    }
   109	    let guarded=loosen_guarded(target,current);
   110	    if !guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){
   111	        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut false);
   112	        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty(){return complete;}
   113	        let current_bridge=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
   114	        if current_bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(){return current_bridge;}
   115	    }
   116	    guarded
   117	}

   430	    pub fn cap(&self, by: &[String]) -> Option<u32> {
   431	        self.constraints.iter().find_map(|constraint| match constraint {
   432	            Constraint::Cap { by: group, max } if group == by && !self.cap_exempts_null(group) => Some(*max),
   433	            _ => None,
   434	        })
   435	    }
   436	
   437	    /// A cap grouping by a `set null` reference does not count its null rows
   438	    /// (LLP 3000.039 §2.2), so it proves no population bound.
   439	    pub fn cap_exempts_null(&self, by: &[String]) -> bool {
   440	        by.iter().any(|column| self.on_delete.get(column) == Some(&references::OnDelete::SetNull))
   441	    }

   303	fn stmt_site(s: &Stmt) -> u32 {
   304	    match s {
   305	        Stmt::As { site, .. }
   306	        | Stmt::Bind { site, .. }
   307	        | Stmt::Expr { site, .. }
   308	        | Stmt::Require { site, .. }
   309	        | Stmt::Return { site, .. }
   310	        | Stmt::Emit { site, .. }
   311	        | Stmt::If { site, .. }
   312	        | Stmt::For { site, .. }
   313	        | Stmt::Break { site }
   314	        | Stmt::Continue { site } => site.line,
   315	    }
   316	}
   317	
   318	fn has_write(e: &crate::ast::Expr) -> bool {
   319	    let mut e = e.clone();
   320	    let mut writes = false;
   321	    crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut e, &mut |e| {
   322	        if matches!(e, crate::ast::Expr::Write { .. }) {
   323	            writes = true;
   324	        }
   325	    });
   326	    writes
   327	}
   328	
   329	// A source printer is deliberately lossy (for example it prints a merge's
   330	// header without its lanes). Security comparison retains every AST field and
   331	// strips only diagnostic locations. Exhaustive Expr matching makes new
   332	// expression forms require an explicit location-normalization decision.
   333	fn expression_signature(value:&crate::ast::Expr)->String {
   334	    use crate::ast::{Expr,ShapeField};
   335	    fn shape(fields:&mut [ShapeField]) {
   336	        for field in fields {
   337	            match field {
   338	                ShapeField::Spread{site}|ShapeField::NamedSpread{site,..}|ShapeField::Bare{site,..}|ShapeField::Field{site,..}=>*site=crate::Site::default(),
   339	            }
   340	        }
   341	    }
   342	    fn read(r:&mut crate::ast::ReadExpr) {
   343	        r.site=crate::Site::default();
   344	        for k in &mut r.keys{k.site=crate::Site::default();}
   345	        for seek in &mut r.seeks{seek.site=crate::Site::default();}
   346	        if let Some(range)=&mut r.range{range.site=crate::Site::default();}
   347	    }
   348	    let mut value=value.clone();
   349	    crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |expr| {
   350	        match expr {
   351	            Expr::Read(r)=>read(r),
   352	            Expr::Exists{read:r,site}|Expr::Created{read:r,site}=>{read(r);*site=crate::Site::default();},
   353	            Expr::Merge(m)=>{
   354	                m.site=crate::Site::default();
   355	                for lane in &mut m.lanes {
   356	                    lane.site=crate::Site::default();
   357	                    if let Some(fields)=&mut lane.project{shape(fields);}
   358	                    if let Some(r)=&mut lane.planned{read(r);}
   359	                    if let Some((_,r))=&mut lane.nulls{read(r);}
   360	                }
   361	            },
   362	            Expr::Object{fields,site}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();for f in fields{f.site=crate::Site::default();}},
   363	            Expr::Shape{fields,site,..}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();shape(fields);},
   364	            Expr::Write{keys,fields,site,..}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();for k in keys{k.site=crate::Site::default();}for f in fields{f.site=crate::Site::default();}},
   365	            Expr::Name{site,..}|Expr::Focal{site}|Expr::Literal{site,..}|Expr::Array{site,..}
   366	            |Expr::Unary{site,..}|Expr::Binary{site,..}|Expr::Member{site,..}|Expr::Call{site,..}
   367	            |Expr::Assert{site,..}|Expr::ShapeUse{site,..}|Expr::Walk{site,..}|Expr::If{site,..}=>*site=crate::Site::default(),
   368	        }
   369	    });
   370	    format!("{value:?}")
   371	}
   372	
   373	fn normalized_statement(s: &Stmt) -> String {
   374	    match s {
   375	        Stmt::As {
   376	            principal, body, ..
   377	        } => format!(
   378	            "as {} {:?}",
   379	            expression_signature(principal),
   380	            body.iter().map(normalized_statement).collect::<Vec<_>>()
   381	        ),
   382	        Stmt::Bind { names, value, .. } => format!("bind {names:?} {}", expression_signature(value)),
   383	        Stmt::Expr { value, .. } => format!("expr {}", expression_signature(value)),
   384	        Stmt::Require {
   385	            condition,
   386	            code,
   387	            message,
   388	            ..
   389	        } => format!(
   390	            "require {} {code:?} {message:?}",
   391	            expression_signature(condition)
   392	        ),
   393	        Stmt::Return { value, .. } => format!("return {}", expression_signature(value)),
   394	        Stmt::Emit {
   395	            effect,
   396	            payload,
   397	            once,
   398	            ..
   399	        } => format!(
   400	            "emit {effect} {} {:?}",
   401	            expression_signature(payload),
   402	            once.as_ref().map(expression_signature)
   403	        ),
   404	        Stmt::If {
   405	            condition,

exec

     7	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
     8	pub struct CalendarKey {
     9	    pub function: String,
    10	    pub column: String,
    11	    pub millis: i64,
    12	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "zero")]
    13	    pub offset: i64,
    14	}
    15	
    16	impl CalendarKey {
    17	    pub fn value(&self, row: &Value) -> Value {
    18	        if self.column.contains('.') && !matches!(row.field(&self.column), Ok(Value::Int(_))) { return Value::Null; }
    19	        let at = row.field(&self.column).ok().and_then(|v| v.as_int().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    20	        Value::Int(match self.function.as_str() {
    21	            // @ref LLP 3000.040#23-index-keys-views-and-windows — civil keys carry no duration; the same function as the builtin.
    22	            "month" => crate::calendar::month_of(at),
    23	            "year" => crate::calendar::year_of(at),
    24	            _ => calendar_ordinal(at, self.millis, self.offset),
    25	        })
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    /// `month` and `year` count civil periods; the others count fixed durations.
    29	    pub fn civil(&self) -> bool { matches!(self.function.as_str(), "month" | "year") }
    30	    pub fn validate(&self, source: &Table) -> Result<(), String> {
    31	        if !source.columns.get(&self.column).is_some_and(|kind| kind == &ColumnType::Time
    32	            || (source.recorded.contains_key(&self.column) && kind.required() == &ColumnType::Time))
    33	            || (self.civil() && (self.millis != 0 || self.offset != 0))
    34	            || (!self.civil() && (self.millis <= 0 || self.offset < 0 || self.offset >= self.millis))
    35	            || !matches!(self.function.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket" | "month" | "year")
    36	            || (self.function == "day" && self.millis != 86_400_000) || (self.function == "hour" && self.millis != 3_600_000) {
    37	            return Err("computed time keys need a required time column; day, hour and bucket take a positive period and a nonnegative offset smaller than it, month and year take neither".into());
    38	        }
    39	        Ok(())
    40	    }
    41	}
    42	
    43	fn zero(value: &i64) -> bool { *value == 0 }
    44	
    45	// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — anchored UTC ordinals use the existing integer key encoding on every host; zero offsets retain the old serialized form.
    46	pub fn calendar_ordinal(at: i64, millis: i64, offset: i64) -> i64 {
    47	    ((i128::from(at) - i128::from(offset)).div_euclid(i128::from(millis))) as i64
    48	}

    50	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
    51	pub enum Operand { Field(String), Literal(Value) }
    52	impl Operand {
    53	    fn value<'a>(&'a self, row: &'a Value, member: Option<&'a Value>) -> &'a Value {
    54	        match self {
    55	            Self::Field(field) => match member.zip(field.strip_prefix("$row.")) {
    56	                Some((member, field)) => member.field(field),
    57	                None => row.field(field),
    58	            }.unwrap_or(&Value::Null),
    59	            Self::Literal(value) => value,
    60	        }
    61	    }
    62	}

   151	/// Bind one table. A refused line becomes a diagnostic and a `deny` (or an
   152	/// absent sync), so one mistake does not cascade into every reader.
   153	fn bind_table(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Table {
   154	    let decl = &info.decl;
   155	    let mut columns = BTreeMap::new();
   156	    columns.insert("id".to_owned(), core::ColumnType::Id);
   157	    for column in &decl.columns {
   158	        if let TypeKind::Ref { table } = &column.type_.kind
   159	            && !catalog.tables.contains_key(table)
   160	        {
   161	            diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site, format!("column {}.{} references unknown table {table}", decl.name, column.name), name_rewrite(table, catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), Some("declare the table, or use id for an opaque identity"))));
   162	        }
   163	        columns.insert(column.name.clone(), scalar_to_column(&column.type_));
   164	    }
   165	    let mut recorded = BTreeMap::new();
   166	    for (path, kind) in &info.recorded {
   167	        let (reference, column) = path.split_once('.').expect("admitted path");
   168	        let TypeKind::Ref { table } = &info.columns[reference].kind else { unreachable!("admitted reference") };
   169	        recorded.insert(path.clone(), core::RecordedField { reference: reference.into(), table: table.clone(), column: column.into() });
   170	        columns.insert(path.clone(), scalar_to_column(kind));
   171	    }
   172	    columns.extend(info.captures.iter().map(|(name, ty)| (name.clone(), scalar_to_column(ty))));
   173	    let mut indexes = BTreeMap::new();
   174	    let mut constraints = Vec::new();
   175	    for index in &info.indexes {
   176	        let mut bound = core::Index::of(&index.columns.iter().map(String::as_str).collect::<Vec<_>>(), index.unique);
   177	        for (position, column) in index.columns.iter().enumerate() {
   178	            if let Some(expr) = index.computed.get(column) {
   179	                match super::time_indexes::key(&decl.name, &info.columns, column, expr) {
   180	                    Ok(key) => bound.components[position] = core::IndexComponent::Calendar(key),
   181	                    Err(_) => {} // The catalog has already located this refusal.
   182	                }
   183	            }
   184	        }
   185	        if let Some((position, fields)) = &index.words {
   186	            bound.components.insert(*position, core::IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields: fields.clone(), contract: serde_json::Value::String(snapback4_core::words::CONTRACT.to_owned()) });
   187	        }
   188	        indexes.insert(index.name.clone(), bound);
   189	        if index.unique && index.name != "byId" {
   190	            constraints.push(Constraint::Unique { index: index.name.clone() });
   191	        }
   192	    }
   193	    let mut rules = Rules::deny();
   194	    for effect in [Effect::Read, Effect::Insert, Effect::Update, Effect::Delete] {
   195	        let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == effect);
   196	        let bound = match (effect, &decl.public, rule) {
   197	            (Effect::Read, Some((reason, _)), Some(rule)) => {
   198	                diagnostics.push(unknown(&rule.site, format!("table {} cannot combine public with an explicit read rule", decl.name), format!("remove the read line, or remove `public '{reason}'`")));
   199	                RuleExpr::Deny
   200	            }
   201	            (Effect::Read, Some((reason, _)), None) => RuleExpr::Public { reason: reason.clone() },
   202	            (_, _, Some(rule)) => match bind_rule(catalog, info, rule) {
   203	                Ok(bound) => bound,
   204	                Err(mut diagnostic) => {
   205	                    if diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP" && diagnostic.cascade_of.is_none() { every_hop(catalog, info, rule, &mut diagnostic); }
